<h1>Generador de certificados para KSE</h1>
<p class="sub">Todo se genera en este navegador, sin conexión. RSA 2048 · SHA-256 · validez 10 años · SAN = CN · exporta .p12, .jks o ZIP.</p>

async function genKey(forge){
  const subtle = (window.crypto && window.crypto.subtle);
  if (subtle) try {
    const kp = await subtle.generateKey({name:"RSASSA-PKCS1-v1_5", modulusLength:2048,
      publicExponent:new Uint8Array([1,0,1]), hash:"SHA-256"}, true, ["sign","verify"]);
    const der = new Uint8Array(await subtle.exportKey("pkcs8", kp.privateKey));
    let s=""; for (const b of der) s+=String.fromCharCode(b);
    const priv = forge.pki.privateKeyFromAsn1(forge.asn1.fromDer(s));
    return {privateKey:priv, publicKey:forge.pki.setRsaPublicKey(priv.n, priv.e)};
  } catch(e) { console.warn("WebCrypto no disponible, uso forge", e); }
  return forge.pki.rsa.generateKeyPair({bits:2048, e:0x10001});
}
async function makeCert(forge, cn, f){
  const keys = await genKey(forge);
  const cert = forge.pki.createCertificate();
  cert.publicKey = keys.publicKey;
  cert.serialNumber = "01" + forge.util.bytesToHex(forge.random.getBytesSync(15));
  const now = new Date(Date.now()-5*60000);
  cert.validity.notBefore = now;
  const end = new Date(now); end.setFullYear(end.getFullYear()+10);
  cert.validity.notAfter = end;
  const attrs = [
    {shortName:"C", value:f.C},{shortName:"ST", value:f.ST},{shortName:"L", value:f.L},
    {shortName:"O", value:f.O},{shortName:"OU", value:f.OU},{shortName:"CN", value:cn}];
  if (f.E) attrs.push({name:"emailAddress", value:f.E});
  cert.setSubject(attrs); cert.setIssuer(attrs);
  cert.setExtensions([
    {name:"basicConstraints", cA:false, critical:true},
    {name:"keyUsage", digitalSignature:true, keyEncipherment:true, critical:true},
    {name:"extKeyUsage", serverAuth:true, clientAuth:true},
    {name:"subjectAltName", altNames:[{type:2, value:cn}]},
    {name:"subjectKeyIdentifier"}]);
  cert.sign(keys.privateKey, forge.md.sha256.create());
  return {cn, cert, key:keys.privateKey};
}
// Un .p12 con una o varias entradas, sin contraseña (password vacía)
function makeP12(forge, entries, password){
  const A = forge.asn1;
  let all = null;
  for (const e of entries){
    const p = forge.pkcs12.toPkcs12Asn1(e.key, [e.cert], password,
      {algorithm:"3des", friendlyName:e.cn, generateLocalKeyId:true});
    if (!all) { all = p; continue; }
    // fusionar contentInfos del authSafe
    const getSeq = pfx => {
      const ci = pfx.value[1]; const octet = ci.value[1].value[0];
      return {octet, seq: A.fromDer(octet.value)};
    };
    const a = getSeq(all), b = getSeq(p);
    a.seq.value.push(...b.seq.value);
    a.octet.value = A.toDer(a.seq).getBytes();
  }
  // recalcular MAC
  if (password !== null) {
    const authSafeBytes = all.value[1].value[1].value[0].value;
    const salt = forge.random.getBytesSync(8), count = 2048;
    const macKey = forge.pkcs12.generateKey(password, forge.util.createBuffer(salt), 3, count, 20);
    const mac = forge.hmac.create(); mac.start("sha1", macKey); mac.update(authSafeBytes);
    const macData = A.create(A.Class.UNIVERSAL, A.Type.SEQUENCE, true, [
      A.create(A.Class.UNIVERSAL, A.Type.SEQUENCE, true, [
        A.create(A.Class.UNIVERSAL, A.Type.SEQUENCE, true, [
          A.create(A.Class.UNIVERSAL, A.Type.OID, false, A.oidToDer(forge.pki.oids.sha1).getBytes()),
          A.create(A.Class.UNIVERSAL, A.Type.NULL, false, "")]),
        A.create(A.Class.UNIVERSAL, A.Type.OCTETSTRING, false, mac.digest().getBytes())]),
      A.create(A.Class.UNIVERSAL, A.Type.OCTETSTRING, false, salt),
      A.create(A.Class.UNIVERSAL, A.Type.INTEGER, false, A.integerToDer(count).getBytes())]);
    all.value[2] = macData;
  }
  return A.toDer(all).getBytes();
}
// Keystore JKS (formato propietario de Java) con varias entradas PrivateKeyEntry
function makeJks(forge, entries, password){
  const sha1 = s => { const m = forge.md.sha1.create(); m.update(s); return m.digest().getBytes(); };
  let pw = ""; for (const ch of password) { const c = ch.charCodeAt(0); pw += String.fromCharCode(c>>8, c&255); }
  const u32 = n => String.fromCharCode((n>>>24)&255,(n>>>16)&255,(n>>>8)&255,n&255);
  const u16 = n => String.fromCharCode((n>>8)&255,n&255);
  const utf = s => { const b = forge.util.encodeUtf8(s); return u16(b.length)+b; };
  const u64 = n => u32(Math.floor(n/4294967296)) + u32(n>>>0);
  const A = forge.asn1;
  let out = u32(0xFEEDFEED) + u32(2) + u32(entries.length);
  const now = Date.now();
  for (const e of entries){
    const plain = A.toDer(forge.pki.wrapRsaPrivateKey(forge.pki.privateKeyToAsn1(e.key))).getBytes();
    const salt = forge.random.getBytesSync(20);
    let digest = salt, enc = "";
    for (let i = 0; i < plain.length; i += 20){
      digest = sha1(pw + digest);
      for (let j = 0; j < 20 && i+j < plain.length; j++)
        enc += String.fromCharCode(plain.charCodeAt(i+j) ^ digest.charCodeAt(j));
    }
    const prot = salt + enc + sha1(pw + plain);
    const epki = A.toDer(A.create(A.Class.UNIVERSAL, A.Type.SEQUENCE, true, [
      A.create(A.Class.UNIVERSAL, A.Type.SEQUENCE, true, [
        A.create(A.Class.UNIVERSAL, A.Type.OID, false, A.oidToDer("1.3.6.1.4.1.42.2.17.1.1").getBytes()),
        A.create(A.Class.UNIVERSAL, A.Type.NULL, false, "")]),
      A.create(A.Class.UNIVERSAL, A.Type.OCTETSTRING, false, prot)])).getBytes();
    const certDer = A.toDer(forge.pki.certificateToAsn1(e.cert)).getBytes();
    out += u32(1) + utf(e.cn.toLowerCase()) + u64(now) + u32(epki.length) + epki
         + u32(1) + utf("X.509") + u32(certDer.length) + certDer;
  }
  return out + sha1(pw + "Mighty Aphrodite" + out);
}
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
// ---- Perfiles (los dos primeros son fijos; el tercero se guarda en este navegador) ----
const PERFILES = [
  {C:"DE", ST:"Nordrhein-Westfalen", L:"Moenchengladbach", O:"Grupo Santander", OU:"Germany", E:"scts-itos@santander.de"},
  {C:"ES", ST:"Santander", L:"Santander", O:"GRUPO SANTANDER", OU:"SCF Internacional", E:""},
  {C:"", ST:"", L:"", O:"", OU:"", E:""},
];
const CAMPOS = ["C","ST","L","O","OU","E"];
const KEY = "gencerts_personalizado_v2";
try { const g = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY)); if (g && typeof g === "object") Object.assign(PERFILES[2], g); } catch(e) {}
let actual = 0;
function cargar(i){
  actual = i;
  CAMPOS.forEach(c => { $(c).value = PERFILES[i][c] || ""; $(c).readOnly = i !== 2; });
  $("perfilInfo").textContent = i === 2 ? "Rellena los campos; se guardan en este navegador." : "Valores fijos. Elige «Personalizado» para usar otros.";
}
CAMPOS.forEach(c => $(c).addEventListener("input", () => {
  if (actual !== 2) return;
  PERFILES[2][c] = $(c).value;
  try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(PERFILES[2])) } catch(e) {}
}));
document.querySelectorAll('input[name=perfil]').forEach(r => r.addEventListener("change", () => cargar(+r.value)));
cargar(0);
$("optJks").addEventListener("change", () => $("jksPwBox").style.display = $("optJks").checked ? "block" : "none");

const hostsList = () => [...new Set($("hosts").value.split(/\r?\n/).map(s=>s.trim()).filter(s=>s && !s.startsWith("#")))];
$("hosts").addEventListener("input", ()=>{ const n=hostsList().length; $("count").textContent = n+" dominio"+(n===1?"":"s"); });
const log = (m,c) => { const d=document.createElement("div"); if(c) d.className=c; d.textContent=m; $("log").appendChild(d); $("log").scrollTop=1e9; };
const toBytes = s => { const u=new Uint8Array(s.length); for(let i=0;i<s.length;i++) u[i]=s.charCodeAt(i)&255; return u; };
function download(name, data, type){
  const blob = data instanceof Blob ? data : new Blob([data], {type:type||"application/octet-stream"});
  const a=document.createElement("a"); a.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob); a.download=name;
  a.textContent = "⬇ Descargar " + name + " (si no se ha descargado solo)";
  a.style.display = "block"; a.style.marginTop = "8px";
  $("log").appendChild(a); a.click();
}
const safe = cn => cn.replace(/\*/g,"wildcard").replace(/[\\/:?"<>|]/g,"_");
const wait = ms => new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,ms));
function makeCsr(e){
  const csr = forge.pki.createCertificationRequest();
  csr.publicKey = forge.pki.setRsaPublicKey(e.key.n, e.key.e);
  csr.setSubject(e.cert.subject.attributes.map(a=>({type:a.type, value:a.value})));
  csr.setAttributes([{name:"extensionRequest", extensions:[{name:"subjectAltName", altNames:[{type:2, value:e.cn}]}]}]);
  csr.sign(e.key, forge.md.sha256.create());
  return forge.pki.certificationRequestToPem(csr);
}
$("go").addEventListener("click", async ()=>{
  const hosts = hostsList();
  if(!hosts.length){ log("Introduce al menos un dominio.","err"); return; }
  const wantP12=$("optP12").checked, wantJks=$("optJks").checked;
  if(!wantP12 && !wantJks && !$("optFiles").checked){ log("Marca al menos una opción de exportación.","err"); return; }
  if(!$("C").value.trim() || !$("O").value.trim()){ log("Rellena al menos C y O.","err"); return; }
  const f = {C:$("C").value.trim(), ST:$("ST").value.trim(), L:$("L").value.trim(), O:$("O").value.trim(), OU:$("OU").value.trim(), E:$("E").value.trim()};
  const jksPw = $("jksPw").value;
  $("go").disabled = true; $("log").textContent = "";
  try{
    const entries = [];
    for(const cn of hosts){ entries.push(await makeCert(forge, cn, f)); log("✓ "+cn); await wait(0); }
    // Nombre: el CN (si hay varios, el primero + cuántos más)
    const base = safe(hosts[0]) + (hosts.length > 1 ? "_y_" + (hosts.length-1) + "_mas" : "");
    const wantFiles = $("optFiles").checked;
    const nOpts = [wantP12, wantJks, wantFiles].filter(Boolean).length;
    if (nOpts === 1 && wantP12) download(base+".p12", toBytes(makeP12(forge, entries, "")), "application/x-pkcs12");
    else if (nOpts === 1 && wantJks) download(base+".jks", toBytes(makeJks(forge, entries, jksPw)));
    else {
      const zip = new JSZip();
      const dir = zip.folder(base);                       // carpeta con el nombre del CN
      if (wantP12) dir.file(base+".p12", toBytes(makeP12(forge, entries, "")));
      if (wantJks) dir.file(base+".jks", toBytes(makeJks(forge, entries, jksPw)));
      if (wantFiles) for (const e of entries){
        const b = safe(e.cn);
        const d = hosts.length > 1 ? dir.folder(b) : dir;  // una subcarpeta por CN
        d.file(b+".p12", toBytes(makeP12(forge, [e], "")));
        if (wantJks) d.file(b+".jks", toBytes(makeJks(forge, [e], jksPw)));
        d.file(b+".crt", forge.pki.certificateToPem(e.cert));
        d.file(b+".key", forge.pki.privateKeyInfoToPem(forge.pki.wrapRsaPrivateKey(forge.pki.privateKeyToAsn1(e.key))));
        d.file(b+".csr", makeCsr(e));
      }
      download(base+".zip", await zip.generateAsync({type:"blob"}), "application/zip");
    }
    log("Listo: "+entries.length+" certificado(s). En KSE: Archivo → Abrir"+(wantJks && jksPw ? " (contraseña del .jks: la que has puesto)." : " y deja la contraseña en blanco."),"ok");
  }catch(err){ console.error(err); log("Error: "+err.message,"err"); }
  finally{ $("go").disabled = false; }
});
</script></body></html>
